def run_optimize(a0, n, time_limit, rng, beta_init=10, beta_end=20000, lp_interval=1000, lp_K=32, use_lp=True):
    S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    a=normalize_sum(a0,n,S)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0; it=0; best_m=inf; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    while time < time_limit:
        it+=1
        c=fft_convolve_self(y)
        m=c.max()
        if m < best_m:
           best_m=m; best_a=y.copy(); last_improve=it
        beta = beta_init * (beta_end/beta_init)**(it/max(1,total_iters?)) # we may not know total it; use time progress
        beta = min(beta_end, beta_init * np.exp(rate*it))
        # gradient selection
        if it % 3 ==0 and it>100: top? maybe use topk_gradient with K = min(128, 16+it//500)
        else lse_gradient
        grad = ...
        gnorm = norm(grad)
        if gnorm small: noise
        step = 1/(2*beta) for lse; for topk step = 1/(max(K,1)*? maybe use 1/(2*beta) where beta=K*10? Could use base=10; step=max(1/(2*beta), 1/(2*K*10))? We'll use FISTA with step = 1/(2*beta) and add floor 1e-5.
        x=project_simplex(y - step*grad,S)
        # momentum
        t_new...
        y=...
        # if no improve for 1000 or it%lp_interval: LP step
        if use_lp and (it-last_improve>500 or it%lp_interval==0) and time remaining>30:
            active = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:lp_K]
            g = lp_improve(y, active, m, n,S, timeout=20)
            if g is not None:
                # line search blends
                accepted=False
                for tt in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5]:
                   q=project_simplex((1-tt)*a + tt*g,S)  # use a current iterate (projected) not y
                   cm=fft_convolve_self(q).max()
                   if cm < best_m*(1+1e-9): # accept if not much worse? Use current m?
                      a=q; y=q; t=1.0
                      accepted=True
                      if cm<best_m: best_m=cm; best_a=q.copy(); last_improve=it
                      break
                if not accepted:
                   # tiny accept if improves current m? maybe
        # if no improve long, noise/restart from best
        if it - last_improve > 2000:
            noise_scale=0.002*S/np.sqrt(n)
            a=project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n), S)
            y=a.copy(); t=1
            last_improve=it
```

LP helper in detail:
```
def lp_improve(a, active, target_m, n, S, time_out=20.0):
    K=len(active)
    if K==0: return None
    # build sparse
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    for r,k in enumerate(active):
        k=int(k)
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(k,n-1)
        if hi<lo: continue
        # coeff = a[k - np.arange(lo,hi+1)]
        idx = k - np.arange(lo, hi+1)
        coeff = a[idx]
        # only include coeff >0? All nonneg; zeros can skip to reduce.
        mask = coeff > 1e-15
        if mask.any():
           rows.extend([r]*mask.sum()) (avoid Python extend many)
```

Let's design initialization based on continuous arcsine plus random smooth perturbation normalized:
```
def init_power(n,p,eps,rng):
    x=np.arange(1,n+1)/n
    base=x**(-p)
    # add small smooth noise: convolve random with Gaussian at several scales
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n); filt = np.convolve(noise, np.exp(-((np.arange(-L,L+1))/L)**2 /2), 'same')
    return base*(1+0.1*filtered) clip
```

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy import sparse
    HAVE_LP=True
except Exception:
    HAVE_LP=False

def fft_convolve_self(a): ...
def project_simplex(v,S): ...

Detailed `run_fista` implementation:
```
def run_fista(a0,n,time_limit,rng,beta_init=10.0,beta_rate=0.004,beta_max=20000.0,lp_K=24,lp_interval=500,use_lp=True,noise_rate=1.0):
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_sum(a0,n,S)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0; it=0; best_m=inf; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    remaining=time_limit
    base_step=1.0/(2.0*max(beta_init,1.0))
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit-2:
       it+=1
       c=fft_convolve_self(y)
       m=float(c.max())
       if m < best_m - 1e-15: best_m=m; best_a=y.copy(); last_improve=it
       # if used LP recently? skip
       beta=min(beta_max, beta_init*np.exp(beta_rate*it))
       # choose gradient: every 4 use topk? maybe
       if it % 4 ==0 or beta > 1000:
           K=int(min(len(c), max(16, 16 + it//1000)))
           grad=topk_gradient(y,c,K,power=1.0)
           # effective beta for step: K/10? use step=1/(max(K,10)*0.5)? Maybe step = 1/(2*K)?
           step=1.0/(max(K,10)*2.0) # =1/(2K)
       else:
           grad=lse_gradient(y,c,beta)
           step=1.0/(2.0*beta)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gnorm < 1e-12: break
       # If step too small? set floor? step = max(step, 1e-6)
       # FISTA extrapolate
       x=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       t_new=0.5*(1.0+np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
       y_new = x + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(x-a)
       y=np.maximum(y_new,0.0)
       a=x
       t=t_new
       # If exact m of a > best_m*1.1 for many? Reset
       if it - last_improve > 1000:
           # noise from best
           noise_scale=0.003*S/np.sqrt(n)*min(1.0, 0.1+it/10000)
           a=project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n), S)
           y=a.copy(); t=1.0
           last_improve=it
       # LP step
       if use_lp and HAVE_LP and (it % lp_interval ==0 or it-last_improve>800) and time.monotonic()-start < time_limit-15:
           topK=lp_K or 24
           active=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:topK]
           g=lp_improve(a, active, m, n, S, timeout=20.0)
           if g is not None:
               # line search from a (last projected)
               best_local=m; best_q=None
               # Use current y? Use a.
               for tt in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5):
                   q=project_simplex((1.0-tt)*a + tt*g, S)
                   cm=float(fft_convolve_self(q).max())
                   if cm < best_local:
                       best_local=cm; best_q=q
               if best_q is not None and best_local < best_m:
                   a=best_q; y=a.copy(); t=1.0; best_m=best_local; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=it
               elif best_q is not None and best_local < m*(1-1e-8):
                   # accept even if not global best? Could help trajectory
                   a=best_q; y=a.copy(); t=1.0
                   if best_local < best_m: best_m=best_local; best_a=a.copy(); last_improve=it
       # final ensure? no
    # Final polish? recompute best_a norm
    return best_a, best_m
```

`lse_gradient` details:
```
def lse_gradient(a,c,beta):
    n=len(a)
    cm=c.max()
    e=np.exp(beta*(c-cm))
    tot=e.sum()
    if tot<1e-300: weights=np.zeros(len(c)); weights[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else: weights=e/tot
    fsize=1 << (len(weights)+n-1).bit_length()
    W=fft(weights,fsize)
    A=fft(a,fsize)
    return 2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
```
This computes gradient of LSE (1/beta) logsum? Since gradient of LSE = ∑ w_k grad b_k. grad b_k is 2 * cross correlation. Yes.

def topk_gradient(a,c,k,power=1.0):
    idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:k]
    w=np.zeros(len(c)); vals=c[idx]; w... normalize; same.
```
If c has negative tiny? weights positive max(vals,1e-30). Good.

`fft_convolve_self`: Ensure size next pow2 at least 2n-1. Use `np.nextafter`? no.
```
def fft_convolve_self(a):
    n=len(a); size=2*n-1; fsize=1<<(size-1).bit_length()
    A=fft(a,fsize); c=np.real(ifft(A*A)); return c[:size]
```
Potential numerical negatives tiny; max okay. For weights, c may be negative tiny; no issue.

`project_simplex` from previous but need S positive. If v contains NaN, set to 0. Use `np.sort(v)[::-1]`.
```
def project_simplex(v,S):
    n=len(v); v=np.asarray(v,float); v[np.isnan(v)]=0; v=np.clip(v,0,None)
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]; cum=np.cumsum(w); idx=np.arange(1,n+1)
    tc=(cum-S)/idx
    valid=tc <= w
    if not np.any(valid): rho=n
    else: rho=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) +1)
    theta=(cum[rho-1]-S)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
This projects onto nonnegative sum S only if S<=sum v; if S>sum v, not projected properly. Our S is sum normalization; after noise, sum may be less. We should ensure if sum(v)<S, simply return v/(sum)*S with clip? The simplex projection formula assumes S ≤ sum(v). If not, all entries positive? Actually projection of v onto sum S, v≥0: if sum v < S, the projection is v + (S-sum)/n (not just scale). But in our steps, v from y-step may have sum not S and could be lower. The formula with valid none? If S > cum[n] (sum), tc=(cum-S)/idx negative <=w, valid all? Wait if S>sum, S larger => cum-S negative, valid true, rho=n, theta=(sum-S)/n negative, v - negative increases all, good. It works: theta negative, max(v-theta) positive. So sum increases. Good.

Normalize_sum:
```
def normalize_sum(a,n,S):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1)
    if a.size != n: a=np.resize? better if larger truncate if smaller pad? We'll handle before.
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=0: a=np.ones(n)*S/n
    else: a *= S/s
    return np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
```
Values small, under 1000.

`upsample_sequence` from parent: if target n different, use linear interpolation. Need preserve sum? Called then normalize.
```
def resample_sequence(a,n):
    m=len(a)
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    if m<n:
       t_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); return np.interp(t_new, np.linspace(0,1,m), a)
    else:
       # down by averaging blocks
       blocks=n; #? Use interpolation simpler: take m points to n points
       return np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,m), a)
    # if n < m? same works.
```
For non-multiple, linear interp okay.

Construct active selection:
```
def select_active(c, top_k, grid_k):
   top=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
   L=len(c)
   if grid_k>0:
      grid=np.linspace(0,L-1,grid_k).astype(int)
      return np.unique(np.concatenate((top,grid)))
   return top
```
This ensures constraints spread.

```
def init_power(n,p):
    i=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    return i**(-p)
def init_exp(n,lam):
    return np.exp(-lam*np.arange(n,dtype=float)/max(1,n-1))
def init_smooth_random(n,rng,scale=1.0):
    # AR(1) low-pass
    x=np.zeros(n); prev=0.0
    phi = 0.98 if n>100 else 0.9
    sigma = np.sqrt(1-phi*phi)
    for i in range(n): prev = phi*prev + sigma*rng.normal(); x[i]=prev
    x = np.maximum(x,0) + 0.1
    return x
```
But Python loop n=16384 for many inits okay. Could vectorize by looping; fine. Or use filter: `x=np.convolve(rng.standard_normal(n), np.exp(-np.arange(100)/10), 'same')`. Simpler and fast:
```
def init_smooth_random(n,rng,scale=1.0):
    length=min(n, max(16, n//50))
    kernel=np.exp(-((np.arange(length)-length//2)/(length//8+1.0))**2)
    x=np.convolve(rng.standard_normal(n), kernel, mode='same')
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)+0.05
    return x*scale
```
Mode same length same. Good. For n small, length. This uses O(n*length) if length n/50 ~300, okay. Could use FFT? no need.

```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
    m=len(a)
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    xold=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    xnew=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    return np.interp(xnew,xold,a)
```

Now, code details for `lp_improve` sparse:
```
def lp_improve(a, active, target_m, n, S, timeout=12.0):
    if not HAVE_LP or target_m <= 0: return None
    active=np.unique(np.asarray(active,dtype=int))
    K=len(active)
    if K==0: return None
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    for r,k in enumerate(active):
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(k,n-1)
        if hi<lo: continue
        # coeff = a[k - (lo..hi)] = a[hi? wait if i=lo, k-i = k-lo <= hi? Need array idx = k - np.arange(lo,hi+1), gives decreasing indices from k-lo down to k-hi, all in 0..n-1. coeff = a[idx]
        idx = k - np.arange(lo, hi+1)
        coeff = a[idx]
        mask = coeff > 1e-14
        if np.any(mask):
            rows.extend([r]*int(mask.sum()))  # using list extend maybe okay but [r]*sum creates list
            # cols.extend(idx[mask].tolist())
            # data.extend(coeff[mask].tolist())
```
Using Python `extend` with lists from `.tolist()`. To avoid many allocations, we can use preallocated arrays? Simpler okay for K=64. `rows.extend([r] * count)` creates list each row, 64* lists, okay.
Then if rows empty return None.
```
L = sparse.coo_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(K,n)).tocsr()
# Build A = hstack(L, -E_tau)
A = L.tolil(); A.resize((K,n+1,))
for r in range(K): A[r,n] = -1.0
A = A.tocsr()
# A_eq
eq_data=np.ones(n,dtype=float); eq_rows=np.zeros(n,dtype=int); eq_cols=np.arange(n,dtype=int)
A_eq=sparse.coo_matrix((eq_data,(eq_rows,eq_cols)), shape=(1,n+1)).tocsr()
c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1.0
bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n + [(0.0,None)]
res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(K), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=np.array([S]), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': timeout, 'mip_rel_gap':0})
if res.status !=0: return None
g=res.x[:n]; tau=float(res.x[n])
g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
g=np.clip(g,0.0,None)
sg=g.sum()
if sg <= 1e-12: return None
g=g*(S/sg)
tau_scaled=tau*(S/sg)
if not np.isfinite(tau_scaled) or tau_scaled >= target_m*(1.0 - 1e-10):
    # maybe still return a small improvement? no
    return None
# Additional: if max a*g for active? Already.
return g
```
But `A_eq` shape 1x(n+1) with last column zero. Good. However equality sum g=S; if bounds U None, variables can be >S but sum fixed. OK.
`linprog` options may not accept `mip_rel_gap`; remove. Use `{'presolve': True, 'time_limit': timeout}`. If HiGHS error due options unsupported? Could catch. Maybe safe no options except `{'presolve': True}`. Time limit should be okay. If `time_limit` unsupported in older SciPy, could fail. We can try with options then catch and retry no options? More code. Maybe use `options={'time_limit': timeout}` is supported in scipy 1.7+. Could be okay. To be robust:
```
try:
    res=linprog(..., options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': timeout})
except Exception:
    res=linprog(...)
```

```

def select_active(c, top_k, grid_k):
    c=np.asarray(c)
    top=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
    if grid_k>0:
       L=len(c)
       grid=np.linspace(0,L-1,grid_k).astype(int)
       active=np.unique(np.concatenate((top,grid)))
    else: active=top
    return active
```

Functions.

`project_simplex`:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if S <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=np.float64)
    tc = (cum - S) / idx
    valid = tc <= w
    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    else:
        rho = n
    theta = (cum[rho-1] - S) / rho
    out = v - theta
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out
```
If `cum` maybe 0 and S positive, tc negative, valid true, theta negative, out positive. Sum maybe S exactly within round.

`normalize_sum`:
```
def normalize_sum(a,n,S):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
   if a.size<n:
      a=np.pad(a,(0,n-a.size))
   elif a.size>n:
      a=a[:n]
   a=np.nan_to_num(a,...)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-15:
      a=np.full(n, S/n, dtype=float)
   else:
      a = a * (S/s)
   return np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
```
Since S small/large, clip after scaling; if S/n >1000? n small maybe; n≥256, S/256~1.4, okay.

`fft_convolve_self`: Ensure `a` contiguous float.
```
def fft_convolve_self(a):
   n=a.size; size=2*n-1; fsize=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
   A=fft(a, fsize)
   c=np.real(ifft(A*A))
   return c[:size]
```
If n=0 not.

`lse_gradient` with fsize as above. Need pass n maybe.
```
def lse_gradient(a,c,beta):
   n=len(a); L=len(c)
   cm=c.max()
   with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore'):
       e=np.exp(beta*(c-cm))
   tot=e.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(tot) or tot < 1e-300:
       w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
   else: w=e/tot
   fsize=1 << (L+n-1).bit_length() # L+n-1
   W=fft(w,fsize); A=fft(a,fsize)
   g=2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
   return g
```
`1 << (L+n-1).bit_length()` if L+n-1 is power of two? For size m, next pow2 is 1 << (m-1).bit_length(), not m.bit_length() if m is power of two? Let's fix. We need next_power_of_two(m). Use `1 << (m - 1).bit_length()`. Previous code used same. For `fft_convolve_self`: m=size. Use `(size - 1).bit_length()`. For gradient m=L+n-1 (actually circular convolution length L+n-1? To avoid alias for first n of correlation of length L and n, linear length L+n-1; use m=L+n-1). So `fsize=1 << (m - 1).bit_length()`.
Similarly previous code had `1 << (len(w) + n - 1).bit_length()`. That would be too large if len+n-1 is power of two? Example m=8, (7).bit_length=3 ->8; (8).bit_length=3 ->8, same? If m=9, (8).bit_length=4 ->16; (9).bit_length=4 ->16. Actually (m).bit_length and (m-1) differ when m is power of two? m=8: m.bit_length=4 ->1<<4=16 wrong; m-1 bit_length=3 ->8. Use m-1. We'll implement helper `next_pow2(m)`.
```
def next_pow2(m):
   if m <=1: return 1
   return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

def topk_gradient(a,c,k,power=1.0):
   n=len(a); L=len(c); k=min(int(k), L)
   if k <=0: return zeros
   if k == L:
      idx=np.arange(L)
   else:
      idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
   w=np.zeros(L)
   vals=np.maximum(c[idx], 1e-300)
   if power !=1: w_idx=np.power(vals,power)
   else: w_idx=vals
   ws=w_idx.sum(); if ws<1e-30: w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
   else: w[idx]=w_idx/ws
   m=L+n-1; fsize=next_pow2(m)
   ...
```

       ...
       break?

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2350.0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0 + max(60.0, budget - 45.0)

    given=None
    try:
       gv=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
       if gv.size >= 64:
          gv=np.nan_to_num(gv, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
          gv=np.clip(gv,0.0,1000.0)
          if gv.sum() > 0.0:
             given = gv
    except Exception:
       given=None

    best_a=None; best_score=np.inf

    def ... cannot nested. Use inline.

    # decide main n
    if given is not None:
       n_given=len(given)
       if n_given < 256: n_main=1024
       elif n_given > 20000: n_main=10000
       else: n_main=n_given
    else:
       n_main=8192
    # ensure power valid
    if not (256 <= n_main <= 20000): n_main=8192

    # main init
    if given is not None:
       if len(given)==n_main: main_a=given.copy()
       else: main_a=resample_sequence(given,n_main)
    else:

def init_smooth_random(n,rng,scale=1.0):
    klen = max(3, min(n, n//40 + 1))
    # ensure odd
    if klen %2 ==0: klen +=1
    centers = np.arange(-(klen//2), klen//2+1)
    kernel = np.exp(-(centers.astype(float)**2) / (2.0 * max(1.0, (klen//4)**2)))
    x = rng.standard_normal(n)
    x = np.convolve(x, kernel, mode='same')
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0) + 0.1
    return x * scale
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

The program uses an accelerated projected-gradient/LSE search together with
periodic active-set linear programming steps.  It starts from the supplied
construction when available, explores a few seeded lengths, and polishes the
best candidate.  All objective scores below are computed on the normalization
sum(a)=sqrt(2n), where the evaluation function equals max(conv(a,a)).
"""
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft

HAVE_LP = False
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy import sparse
    HAVE_LP = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_LP = False


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def sanitize(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=S}."""
    v = sanitize(v)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if S <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    tc = (cum - S) / idx
    valid = tc <= w
    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    else:
        rho = n
    theta = (cum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    out = v - theta
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def normalize_sum(a, n, S):
    a = sanitize(a).reshape(-1)
    if a.size < n:
        a = np.concatenate([a, np.zeros(n - a.size, dtype=np.float64)])
    elif a.size > n:
        a = a[:n]

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (S / s)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)


def fft_convolve_self(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution via FFT."""
    a = sanitize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = next_pow2(size)
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    return c[:size]


def lse_gradient(a, c, beta):
    """Gradient of the log-sum-exp surrogate of max(c)."""
    n = a.size
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cm = float(np.max(c))
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore"):
        e = np.exp(beta * (c - cm))
    tot = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(tot) or tot < 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / tot

    m = L + n - 1
    fsize = next_pow2(m)
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return g


def topk_gradient(a, c, k, power=1.0):
    """Gradient of a weighted sum of the largest convolution coefficients."""
    n = a.size
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    k = min(int(k), L)
    if k <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if k == L:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]

    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    vals = np.maximum(c[idx], 1e-300)
    if power != 1.0:
        wvals = np.power(vals, float(power))
    else:
        wvals = vals
    ws = float(wvals.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws < 1e-300:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w[idx] = wvals / ws

    m = L + n - 1
    fsize = next_pow2(m)
    W = fft(w, fsize)
    A = fft(a, fsize)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))[:n]
    return g


def select_active(c, top_k, grid_k):
    c = sanitize(c)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=int)
    top_k = min(int(top_k), L)
    grid_k = min(int(grid_k), L)

    if top_k > 0:
        if top_k == L:
            top = np.arange(L)
        else:
            top = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
    else:
        top = np.zeros(0, dtype=int)

    if grid_k > 0:
        grid = np.linspace(0.0, L - 1, grid_k).astype(int)
    else:
        grid = np.zeros(0, dtype=int)

    if top.size == 0:
        out = grid
    elif grid.size == 0:
        out = top
    else:
        out = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, grid)))
    return out


def lp_improve(a, active, target_m, n, S, timeout=12.0):
    """
    Solve min tau s.t. (a*g)_k <= tau for selected active lags,
    g >= 0, sum(g)=S, g_i <= U.
    Returns a normalized g if it actively lowers the constrained maximum.
    """
    if not HAVE_LP or n <= 0 or target_m <= 0:
        return None

    active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
    active = active[(active >= 0) & (active < (2 * n - 1))]
    active = np.unique(active)
    K = active.size
    if K == 0:
        return None

    rows = []
    cols = []
    data = []

    for r, k in enumerate(active):
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(k, n - 1)
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        idx = k - np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
        coeff = a[idx]
        mask = coeff > 1e-14
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt == 0:
            continue
        rows.extend([r] * cnt)
        cols.extend(idx[mask].tolist())
        data.extend(coeff[mask].tolist())

    if not data:
        return None

    L = sparse.coo_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(K, n)).tocsr()

    tau_col = sparse.lil_matrix((K, 1))
    for r in range(K):
        tau_col[r, 0] = -1.0
    tau_col = tau_col.tocsr()

    A = sparse.hstack((L, tau_col), format="csr")

    eq_data = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    eq_rows = np.zeros(n, dtype=int)
    eq_cols = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    A_eq = sparse.coo_matrix((eq_data, (eq_rows, eq_cols)), shape=(1, n + 1)).tocsr()

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0

    max_a = float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 0.0
    U = max(50.0 * S / float(n), 50.0 * max_a, 1e-12)
    bounds = [(0.0, U)] * n + [(0.0, None)]

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64),
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=np.array([S], dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(timeout)},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64),
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=np.array([S], dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

    if getattr(res, "status", 1) != 0:
        return None
    if res.x is None:
        return None

    g = sanitize(res.x[:n])
    tau = float(res.x[n]) if np.isfinite(res.x[n]) else np.inf
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    sg = float(g.sum())
    if sg <= 1e-12:
        return None
    g = g * (S / sg)
    tau_scaled = tau * (S / sg)
    if not np.isfinite(tau_scaled):
        return None
    if tau_scaled > target_m:
        return None

    # Numerical cleanup; keep sum S.
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    sg2 = float(g.sum())
    if sg2 <= 1e-12:
        return None
    g = g * (S / sg2)
    return g


def run_fista(a0, n, time_limit, rng, beta_init=30.0, beta_rate=0.004, beta_max=50000.0,
              lp_top=28, lp_grid=44, use_lp=True):
    """
    Projected accelerated search for max convolution with optional LP polish.
    The sequence is maintained with sum sqrt(2n), so c.max() is the objective.
    """
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = normalize_sum(a0, n, S)
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0

    c0 = fft_convolve_self(a)
    best_m = max(0.0, float(np.max(c0))) if c0.size else 0.0
    best_a = a.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()
    if time_limit <= 5.0:
        return best_a, best_m

    last_good = time.monotonic()
    last_lp = time.monotonic()
    ip = 0

    max_lp = 0
    lp_wait = 1e12
    if use_lp and HAVE_LP:
        max_lp = int(max(0, time_limit / 60.0))
        if n > 12000:
            max_lp = max(0, max_lp // 2)
        if max_lp > 0:
            lp_wait = max(25.0, time_limit / float(max_lp))

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() - start < max(0.0, time_limit - 8.0):
        it += 1
        ip += 1

        sy = float(y.sum())
        if sy < 1e-12:
            y = a.copy()
        else:
            y = y * (S / sy)
            y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)

        c = fft_convolve_self(y)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        m = max(0.0, float(np.max(c)))
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            a = best_a.copy()
            y = a.copy()
            t = 1.0
            continue

        if m < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m
            best_a = y.copy()
            last_good = time.monotonic()

        beta = min(beta_max, beta_init * np.exp(beta_rate * it))

        if beta > 6000.0:
            K = min(64, 16 + int(beta // 1000))
            grad = topk_gradient(y, c, K, 1.0)
            step = max(1.0 / (10.0 * max(16, K)), 1e-4)
        else:
            grad = lse_gradient(y, c, beta)
            step = max(1.0 / (2.0 * beta), 5e-5)

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-12:
            noise = 0.005 * S / np.sqrt(n)
            a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), S)
            y = a.copy()
            t = 1.0
            last_good = time.monotonic()
            if it > 2000:
                break
            continue

        x = project_simplex(y - step * grad, S)

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        t_new = min(50.0, t_new)
        y_new = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - a)
        y_new = np.maximum(y_new, 0.0)
        sy2 = float(y_new.sum())
        if sy2 < 1e-12:
            y = a.copy()
        else:
            y = y_new * (S / sy2)

        a = x
        t = t_new

        now = time.monotonic()

        # Escape local stagnation by restarting around the best point.
        stagnation_threshold = min(120.0, max(30.0, 0.18 * time_limit))
        if now - last_good > stagnation_threshold:
            noise = 0.004 * S / np.sqrt(n)
            a = project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), S)
            y = a.copy()
            t = 1.0
            last_good = now

        # Periodic active-set LP move.
        if (use_lp and HAVE_LP and max_lp > 0
                and ip % max(1, max(3, int(n // 16384))) == 0
                and (now - last_lp) > lp_wait
                and ip < max_lp
                and time_limit - (now - start) > 25.0):
            base = sanitize(y)
            sb = float(base.sum())
            if sb <= 1e-12:
                base = a.copy()
            else:
                base = base * (S / sb)
                base = np.maximum(base, 0.0)

            cb = fft_convolve_self(base)
            mb = max(0.0, float(np.max(cb))) if cb.size else 0.0
            if np.isfinite(mb) and mb < best_m - 1e-15:
                best_m = mb
                best_a = base.copy()
                last_good = now

            active = select_active(cb, lp_top, lp_grid)
            lp_timeout = min(10.0, max(3.0, 0.35 * lp_wait))
            if n > 15000:
                lp_timeout = min(lp_timeout, 7.0)

            g = lp_improve(base, active, mb, n, S, timeout=lp_timeout)
            ip += 1
            last_lp = now

            if g is not None:
                best_local = mb
                best_q = None
                for tt in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.30, 0.45):
                    q = project_simplex((1.0 - tt) * base + tt * g, S)
                    cq = fft_convolve_self(q)
                    cm = max(0.0, float(np.max(cq))) if cq.size else 0.0
                    if np.isfinite(cm) and cm < best_local - 1e-14:
                        best_local = cm
                        best_q = q

                if best_q is not None and best_local < mb - 1e-12:
                    a = best_q
                    y = a.copy()
                    t = 1.0
                    last_good = now
                    if best_local < best_m - 1e-15:
                        best_m = best_local
                        best_a = a.copy()

    best_a = normalize_sum(best_a, n, S)
    final_c = fft_convolve_self(best_a)
    final_m = max(0.0, float(np.max(final_c))) if final_c.size else best_m
    if np.isfinite(final_m) and final_m < best_m:
        best_m = final_m
    return best_a, best_m


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = sanitize(a).reshape(-1)
    m = a.size
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if m <= 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a.mean()), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, a)


def init_power(n, p):
    x = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.power(x, -float(p))


def init_exp_decay(n, lam):
    return np.exp(-float(lam) * np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n - 1))


def init_smooth_random(n, rng, scale=1.0):
    klen = max(3, min(n, n // 40 + 1))
    if klen % 2 == 0:
        klen += 1
    half = klen // 2
    centers = np.arange(-half, half + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    sigma = max(1.0, half / 3.0)
    kernel = np.exp(-(centers * centers) / (2.0 * sigma * sigma))
    x = rng.standard_normal(n)
    x = np.convolve(x, kernel, mode="same")
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0) + 0.1
    return x * float(scale)


def try_evaluate(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        if val is None:
            return np.inf
        val = float(val)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return val
    except Exception:
        pass
    return np.inf


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2350.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget - 45.0)

    given = None
    try:
        gv = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if gv.size >= 64:
            gv = sanitize(gv)
            gv = np.clip(gv, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(gv.sum()) > 0.0:
                given = gv
    except Exception:
        given = None

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf

    # Main length: prefer the supplied construction.
    if given is not None:
        n_given = len(given)
        if n_given < 256:
            n_main = 1024
        elif n_given > 20000:
            n_main = 16384
        else:
            n_main = n_given
    else:
        n_main = 8192
    if not (256 <= n_main <= 20000):
        n_main = 8192

    if given is not None:
        if n_main == len(given):
            main_a = given.copy()
        else:
            main_a = resample_sequence(given, n_main)
    else:
        main_a = 0.85 * init_power(n_main, 0.5) + 0.15 * init_smooth_random(n_main, rng, 1.0)

    # Phase 1: main optimization on the primary length/candidate.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 60:
        phase_time = min(max(60.0, 0.52 * rem), 1700.0)
        if phase_time > 30:
            a1, s1 = run_fista(
                main_a, n_main, phase_time, rng,
                beta_init=30.0, beta_rate=0.004, beta_max=50000.0,
                lp_top=28, lp_grid=44, use_lp=True
            )
            if np.isfinite(s1):
                best_a, best_score = a1, s1
            print("[phase1] n=%d score=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" %
                  (n_main, best_score, time.monotonic() - t0), flush=True)

    # Phase 2: polished refinement of the best sequence found so far.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if best_a is not None and rem > 70:
        n_best = len(best_a)
        phase_time = min(max(45.0, 0.26 * rem), 800.0)
        if phase_time > 30:
            a2, s2 = run_fista(
                best_a, n_best, phase_time, rng,
                beta_init=300.0, beta_rate=0.0025, beta_max=80000.0,
                lp_top=18, lp_grid=32, use_lp=True
            )
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_a, best_score = a2, s2
            print("[phase2] n=%d score=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" %
                  (n_best, best_score, time.monotonic() - t0), flush=True)

    # Phase 3: try one alternate length, usually larger if feasible.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if best_a is not None and rem > 160:
        n_best = len(best_a)
        n_alt = None
        for cand in (2 * n_best, n_best // 2, 8192, 4096, 16384):
            if 1024 <= cand <= 20000 and cand != n_best:
                n_alt = cand
                break
        if n_alt is not None:
            if given is not None and len(given) == n_alt:
                alt_a = given.copy()
            elif best_a is not None:
                alt_a = resample_sequence(best_a, n_alt)
            else:
                alt_a = init_power(n_alt, 0.5)
            phase_time = min(max(60.0, 0.34 * rem), 1100.0)
            if phase_time > 40:
                a3, s3 = run_fista(
                    alt_a, n_alt, phase_time, rng,
                    beta_init=60.0, beta_rate=0.0035, beta_max=40000.0,
                    lp_top=24, lp_grid=40, use_lp=True
                )
                if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_a, best_score = a3, s3
            print("[phase3] n=%d score=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" %
                  (len(best_a) if best_a is not None else 0, best_score, time.monotonic() - t0), flush=True)

    # Phase 4: a few diverse seeds at the current best length.
    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if best_a is None:
        n_seed = 8192
        best_a = init_power(n_seed, 0.5)
        best_score = 2.0

    n_best = len(best_a)
    inits = []
    inits.append((0.80 * init_power(n_best, 0.5) + 0.20 * init_exp_decay(n_best, 1.2)))
    inits.append(init_smooth_random(n_best, rng, 1.0))
    if given is not None:
        inits.append(np.flip(resample_sequence(given, n_best)))
        inits.append(np.sort(resample_sequence(given, n_best)))

    for ii, ai in enumerate(inits):
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 55:
            break
        phase_time = min(max(35.0, 0.30 * rem), 520.0)
        if phase_time < 25:
            break
        a4, s4 = run_fista(
            ai, n_best, phase_time, rng,
            beta_init=80.0, beta_rate=0.0035, beta_max=60000.0,
            lp_top=20, lp_grid=36, use_lp=True
        )
        if np.isfinite(s4) and s4 < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_a, best_score = a4, s4
        print("[phase4.%d] n=%d score=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" %
              (ii, n_best, best_score, time.monotonic() - t0), flush=True)

    # Final safety and verification.
    if best_a is None:
        if given is not None:
            best_a = given.copy()
        else:
            best_a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    best_a = normalize_sum(best_a, len(best_a), np.sqrt(2.0 * len(best_a)))
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    final_seq = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    val = try_evaluate(final_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        if given is not None:
            best_a = normalize_sum(given, len(given), np.sqrt(2.0 * len(given)))
            best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
            final_seq = [float(x) for x in best_a]
            val = try_evaluate(final_seq)

        if not np.isfinite(val):
            # Guaranteed finite fallback.
            n = 1024
            best_a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
            final_seq = [float(x) for x in best_a]
            val = try_evaluate(final_seq)

    print("[final] len=%d best_internal=%.6f evaluator=%.6f" %
          (len(final_seq), best_score, val), flush=True)
    return final_seq
```
